Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the petrography space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 831 and the convergence rate is O(752/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the petrography decomposition. By minimality, the element a^831 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 752-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the petrography space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 500 and the convergence rate is O(487/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 500 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the petrography structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 487. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
